required"},{"author":"main","ts":"2026-09-25T17:52:51.504Z","text":"Review of 8f5530a by Faro (c164bf8b): CHANGES REQUESTED. Address every blocker/major finding below on the SAME branch, then send a new finish report so review re-runs.\n- [major] agent-orchestration/topology/lib/delegation.mjs:40 — The operator-only rule is only a check that AO_AGENT_ID is unset, and the grantor is taken from USER. Any agent session, including the grantee lead, which now runs auto-approved by default since TM-214, can run the grant with AO_AGENT_ID removed from its environment, or append to the delegations JSON file directly. The result is a record claiming the operator granted authority the lead gave itself. That is the self-approval this task exists to remove, now carrying false operator attribution, and it is exactly what the harness safety check was refusing. FIX: Require a channel an agent cannot drive. For example: an interactive TTY with a typed confirmation of the grantee id and scopes, and refusal when any agent marker is present (AO_AGENT_ID, TM_SESSION_ID, CLAUDECODE, a registered agent pane). Or issue grants through the session-host capability channel that agents cannot reach. Record the channel evidence in the grant. If a same-user agent cannot be fully excluded, say so plainly in the docs and in the grant record, not operator-only.\n- [major] agent-orchestration/topology/lib/management.mjs:482 — When integrate runs under a delegation, the recorded actor is the OS user, not the lead that exercised the grant. The merge record then reads actor and delegated_by as the same operator, and nothing shows that the lead acted. Criterion 2 and the task body require actor to be the lead alongside delegated_by and delegation_id. FIX: When fresh.delegation is used, set actor to the caller AO_AGENT_ID, which is the grantee the delegation matched, and assert it in the test. In record-landing, refuse or override a supplied actor that differs from the grantee when authority comes from a delegation."},{"author":"worker:tmux","ts":"2026-09-25T17:53:05.229Z","text":"tmux worker exited after submitting its revision; independent review and integration remain required"},{"author":"main","ts":"2026-09-25T18:10:09.483Z","text":"{\"event\":\"finish\",\"at\":\"2026-09-25T18:10:09.367Z\",\"owner\":\"1b07de2e-6b73-47c6-ad14-aa29eeea67fd\",\"report\":{\"revision\":\"2edd178e9824dd4fa90c88fa57d7ca721db36f22\",\"artifacts\":[\"agent-orchestration/topology/lib/delegation.mjs\",\"agent-orchestration/topology/lib/management.mjs\",\"agent-orchestration/topology/cli.mjs\",\"agent-orchestration/tests/unit/topology-delegation.test.mjs\",\"agent-orchestration/tests/unit/topology-management.test.mjs\",\"agent-orchestration/docs/repository-leads.md\",\"agent-orchestration/CHANGELOG.md\",\"agent-orchestration/dist/cli.cjs\",\"agent-orchestration/dist/mcp.cjs\"],\"checks\":[\"node --test tests/unit/topology-delegation.test.mjs: 10/10 pass\",\"node --test tests/unit/topology-*.test.mjs: 519/520 pass; the one failure (bind adopts a live worker) also fails on untouched 8f5530a and depends on the live tmux environment\",\"npm run build:check: exit 0\",\"claude plugin validate ./agent-orchestration: passed, expected 'No version specified' warning only\",\"CLI smoke: delegate grant from an agent shell refused TOPOLOGY_DELEGATION_OPERATOR_ONLY; with a clean env and no TTY refused TOPOLOGY_DELEGATION_TTY\"],\"evidence\":\"Review round 1 (Faro, 8f5530a) findings addressed. Finding 1: grant needs an interactive TTY plus a typed confirmation of '<grantee> <scopes>'. grant and revoke refuse AO_AGENT_ID, TM_SESSION_ID, CLAUDECODE, CLAUDE_CODE_*, CODEX_* and a tmux pane the census binds to an agent. Grants record channel evidence and an assurance field stating a same-OS-user agent is NOT excluded. Events are HMAC-signed (mode-600 key), and integrate/record-landing refuse a file with any unsigned, edited or evidence-less event. Finding 2: under a delegation, authorization.actor = grantee (the caller's AO_AGENT_ID), with delegated_by and delegation_id alongside. A differing --actor is refused with TOPOLOGY_DELEGATION_ACTOR in both integrate and record-landing, and tests assert both.\",\"risks\":[\"A deliberate agent running as the same OS user can still forge a grant: it can unset the markers, drive a pty (script or tmux send-keys) and read the mode-600 key. This is documented and recorded in each grant's assurance field. Full exclusion needs a different-uid key holder or the session-host capability channel, which is out of scope here.\",\"Round-1 grant records (no mac or channel) are now refused. Any existing delegations file must be re-granted.\",\"Toward the approved plan-approval work: record-landing no longer needs --actor under a delegation, so refusing --actor inside managed sessions later is a one-line change.\"]},\"state\":\"ready-for-review\"}"},{"author":"main","ts":"2026-09-25T18:10:10.469Z","text":"{\"event\":\"review-queued\",\"request\":{\"base_revision\":\"9a65052ee20cde632fc5654aa8f1f66bd83a1e1c\",\"patch_path\":\"/home/ryan/.local/state/bytedesk/agent-orchestration/reviewers/inboxes/8890762dbb279261/requests/TM-234-2edd178e9824dd4fa90c88fa57d7ca721db36f22.patch\",\"patch_sha256\":\"53f9a97f2418049adf4d112e4661ebfebd9945b611284950dd5c62ca85fa7fac\",\"nonce\":\"0ebd817a-288a-4cbd-916a-8e1f74dcae8c\",\"task\":\"TM-234\",\"revision\":\"2edd178e9824dd4fa90c88fa57d7ca721db36f22\",\"repo_id\":\"/home/ryan/Documents/GitHub/ByteDeskAI/bytedesk-marketplace/.git\",\"reviewer_id\":\"c164bf8b\",\"binding\":{\"serverKey\":\"/tmp/tmux-1000/default\",\"serverPid\":25595,\"sessionId\":\"$288\",\"sessionCreated\":1790282302,\"paneId\":\"%502\",\"panePid\":2543160},\"author_agent_ids\":[\"1b07de2e-6b73-47c6-ad14-aa29eeea67fd\"],\"created_at\":\"2026-09-25T18:10:09.785Z\",\"delivery\":{\"rang\":true,\"at\":\"2026-09-25T18:10:10.340Z\"},\"state\":\"published\",\"path\":\"/home/ryan/.local/state/bytedesk/agent-orchestration/reviewers/inboxes/8890762dbb279261/requests/TM-234-2edd178e9824dd4fa90c88fa57d7ca721db36f22.json\"},\"blocked\":null}"},{"author":"worker:tmux","ts":"2026-09-25T18:10:38.400Z","text":"tmux worker exited after submitting its revision; independent review and integration remain required"},{"author":"worker:tmux","ts":"2026-09-25T18:13:18.292Z","text":"tmux worker exited after submitting its revision; independent review and integration remain required"},{"author":"main","ts":"2026-09-25T18:13:28.153Z","text":"{\"event\":\"finish\",\"at\":\"2026-09-25T18:13:28.046Z\",\"owner\":\"1b07de2e-6b73-47c6-ad14-aa29eeea67fd\",\"report\":{\"revision\":\"92bb318d8c3a9fb8db1d84cec2f9de3e7f8a2de1\",\"artifacts\":[\"agent-orchestration/topology/lib/delegation.mjs\",\"agent-orchestration/topology/lib/management.mjs\",\"agent-orchestration/topology/cli.mjs\",\"agent-orchestration/tests/unit/topology-delegation.test.mjs\",\"agent-orchestration/tests/unit/topology-management.test.mjs\",\"agent-orchestration/docs/repository-leads.md\",\"agent-orchestration/CHANGELOG.md\",\"agent-orchestration/dist/cli.cjs\",\"agent-orchestration/dist/mcp.cjs\"],\"checks\":[\"node --test tests/unit/topology-delegation.test.mjs: 11/11 pass\",\"node --test tests/unit/topology-*.test.mjs: 520/521 pass; the one failure ('bind adopts a live worker') also fails on untouched 8f5530a and passes with env -u TMUX -u TMUX_PANE, so it is an artifact of the live tmux environment\",\"npm run build:check: exit 0\",\"claude plugin validate ./agent-orchestration: passed, with only the expected 'No version specified' warning\",\"Live check: delegate grant run with env -i from inside this Claude Code session was refused with TOPOLOGY_DELEGATION_OPERATOR_ONLY (agent ancestor ~/.local/share/claude/versions/2.1.280)\"],\"evidence\":\"Addresses Faro's round-1 review of 8f5530a. The lead (d60f0608) narrowed the fix. FINDING 1 (grant channel): grant refuses any agent marker (AO_AGENT_ID, TM_SESSION_ID, CLAUDECODE, CLAUDE_CODE_*, CODEX_*), a Claude Code or Codex ancestor process, and a tmux pane the census binds to an agent. It also requires an interactive TTY and a typed confirmation '<grantee> <scopes>'. revoke has the same refusals, without the confirmation. The grant records the checks it passed as channel evidence: kind 'interactive-same-user', agent_proof false, and a note that it is not agent-proof against a same-uid agent, which can drive a TTY through tmux or script. integrate and record-landing refuse a delegations file holding any grant without that evidence. HMAC signing was deliberately dropped: the key would be readable by the same uid, so a signature added nothing and would have looked like proof. repository-leads.md says all of this and names a different-uid grant store or a session-host capability channel as future work. That architecture decision belongs to the marketplace lead and the operator. FINDING 2 (actor): under a delegation, authorization.actor is the grantee (the caller's AO_AGENT_ID), with delegated_by and delegation_id alongside. integrate and record-landing refuse an --actor that differs from the grantee (TOPOLOGY_DELEGATION_ACTOR), and tests assert both.\",\"risks\":[\"NOT agent-proof against a same-OS-user agent. Such an agent can unset the markers, start a shell outside its own process tree, drive a TTY through tmux or script, or write the delegations file directly with copied evidence fields. The checks stop ordinary-path use only. This is stated in every grant and in the docs.\",\"Grants made by round-1 code carry no channel evidence and are now refused, so any existing grant must be re-granted.\",\"Toward the approved plan-approval work: record-landing no longer needs --actor under a delegation, so refusing --actor inside managed sessions later is a one-line change.\"]},\"state\":\"ready-for-review\"}"},{"author":"main","ts":"2026-09-25T18:13:28.804Z","text":"{\"event\":\"review-queued\",\"request\":{\"base_revision\":\"9a65052ee20cde632fc5654aa8f1f66bd83a1e1c\",\"patch_path\":\"/home/ryan/.local/state/bytedesk/agent-orchestration/reviewers/inboxes/8890762dbb279261/requests/TM-234-92bb318d8c3a9fb8db1d84cec2f9de3e7f8a2de1.patch\",\"patch_sha256\":\"c80a9ac9c00c86ff9b9afd90f709b7165a971d605036e8ce3de488b8efc9c133\",\"nonce\":\"e50de10a-e13a-4c01-b5ab-341e62bbb21f\",\"task\":\"TM-234\",\"revision\":\"92bb318d8c3a9fb8db1d84cec2f9de3e7f8a2de1\",\"repo_id\":\"/home/ryan/Documents/GitHub/ByteDeskAI/bytedesk-marketplace/.git\",\"reviewer_id\":\"c164bf8b\",\"binding\":{\"serverKey\":\"/tmp/tmux-1000/default\",\"serverPid\":25595,\"sessionId\":\"$288\",\"sessionCreated\":1790282302,\"paneId\":\"%502\",\"panePid\":2543160},\"author_agent_ids\":[\"1b07de2e-6b73-47c6-ad14-aa29eeea67fd\"],\"created_at\":\"2026-09-25T18:13:28.518Z\",\"delivery\":{\"rang\":false,\"reason\":\"a failure word on the pane turned out to be a path; closed: "2026-09-27T03:13:40.684Z"
---

Operator request 2026-09-24: the repository lead must be able to authorize integration itself, without the operator running each command.

Today `ao-topology manage integrate --authorized` and `manage record-landing --authorized` take a bare flag. When the lead passes it, the lead is attesting to an authority it grants itself. Claude Code's auto-mode safety check refused exactly that as "Self-Approval" when lead d60f0608 tried to record the landings of TM-214, TM-215, TM-221 and TM-224. The operator had to run the command by hand.

The fix is a standing delegation that the operator grants once, and the lead then exercises, with both recorded.
- `ao-topology delegate grant --to <agent-id> --repo <consumer> --scope integrate,record-landing [--expires <duration>] [--reason <text>]`
  - Run by the operator, never by the grantee: refuse when AO_AGENT_ID equals the grantee, or when the caller is a managed agent session.
  - Writes an append-only record under the state home: grantor (OS user), grantee, repo_id, scopes, created_at, expires_at, and an id.
- `ao-topology delegate list|revoke <id>`, run by the operator.
- When `manage integrate` and `record-landing` run without an explicit operator `--authorized`, they accept a valid, unexpired grant covering the caller's agent id, the repo and the scope.
  - The merge record stores `authorization {actor: <lead>, delegated_by: <grantor>, delegation_id, authorized: true}`.
  - `governedCompletion` keeps its current checks; it may display `delegated_by`.
- Every use is journaled and appears in task evidence.
- The grant never covers deploy, publish, push or spend. Those keep their separate authorization.
- Document the grant command in docs/repository-leads.md, so the operator can pair it with a Claude Code permission rule for the grant-exercising command if they choose.
